# Tasks

Status values: `TODO`, `IN PROGRESS`, `AWAITING VALIDATION`, `DONE`, `BLOCKED`. Add an owner and branch when work starts, and evidence when it ends. Each Phase 0 row is a package in the [action plan](docs/ACTION_PLAN.md), which has its sources, steps and acceptance.

## Bootstrap

| ID | Status | Task and acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| B01 | DONE | Private repository, licences and notices, contributor guides, plan and decisions, a Rust workspace with one placeholder crate, the check script and CI. No server behavior. |
| B02 | DONE | CI passed on Linux, Windows and macOS for the bootstrap commit `24746aa` ([run 44043347533](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44043347533)). |
| B03 | DONE | Licence changed to GPL-3.0-or-later (D4) so Aurora Viewer's crates can be reused (D11); reference checkouts pinned in docs/REFERENCES.md; Phase 0 action plan written. |
| B04 | TODO | Contributor onboarding for volunteers: label starter tasks, and decide how task claims are made (issue, PR or this file). |

## Prerequisite in Tranquillity (C#)

Tracked in the Tranquillity fork. The S and M packages are in [SLUA_TRANQUILLITY.md](docs/SLUA_TRANQUILLITY.md); the T.A rows come from [ASSET_SYSTEM.md](docs/ASSET_SYSTEM.md) and go to OpenSim-NGC as pull requests.

| ID | Status | Task and acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| S0.1 | TODO | Build Linden's engine as a shared library for Linux and Windows in CI; its harness runs a sample LSL and SLua script. |
| S0.2 | TODO | C interface over Executor, reviewed header first. |
| S0.3 | TODO | .NET wrapper; an LSL script calls `llOwnerSay` from C# on Linux and Windows; empty host call timed. |
| S1.1 | TODO | Bindings generated from `ISystemAPI`; all 831 functions callable from LSL and SLua. |
| S1.2 | TODO | Compiler patch and `ll`, `ossl`, `bot` tables, with `OsslGate`. |
| S1.3 | TODO | Delays and deferred calls as yields; Phlox's syscall and sleep tests pass. |
| M1 | TODO | Portable script state spec (LLSD, versioned), YEngine exporter, `script migrate audit` dry run on a copy of real grid state. No behaviour change. |
| M2 | TODO | Phlox exporter and importer, YEngine importer; corpus round trips with no `state_entry`. |
| M3 | TODO | SLua engine imports and exports portable state (after S2). |
| M4 | TODO | Migrator: three engines, `FallbackScriptEngines`, one owner per script, compile and risk checks, round trip, quiet-point switch, rollback window. |
| M5 | TODO | Hypergrid: `<Variables>`-form state for LSL on SLua, travel copy for SLua scripts, arrivals with YEngine or XEngine state; checked on the live harness against core OpenSim. |
| T.A1 | TODO | FSAssets fixes as OpenSim-NGC PRs, one per row: TB22 (a failed row insert answers as failed; names and descriptions keep only what a `utf8` table holds), TB23 (a missing, empty, unreadable or mismatching file is answered as missing; storing matching content rewrites a damaged file), TB24 (the writer writes a temporary file, forces it to disk and renames it, and retries instead of ending the process; a warning when `SpoolDirectory` is under `/tmp`) and TB25 (`AssetsExist` checks the file). Each with a test that fails on `develop`. The fault probe from the 21 November 2026 study is the acceptance check on MariaDB and PostgreSQL. |
| T.A2 | TODO | Hypergrid content as OpenSim-NGC PRs (TB26, Hypergrid V2 §14.3): the example files state the real `OutboundPermission` default, and a visitor is told when content they take, upload or edit will not be sent home. Ferrobot's `hg-content` scenario passes with both settings and shows the message. |
| T.A3 | TODO | The read-only FSAssets audit (counts by class, type and month; no ids unless asked) under Tranquillity `tools/`, so operators can measure their store before any clean-up or move. |

## Phase 0: Foundations

Gate: the protocol crate decodes and re-encodes every packet captured from a C# region session byte for byte, LLSD round-trips in all three encodings against the C# encoder, and a C# region runs against Ferroan's asset service, all checked by the live harness (P0.10a to P0.10e); then the maintainer's Firestorm check (G0).

| ID | Status | Depends on | Task and acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| P0.1 | DONE | | C# oracle in `tools/reference-oracle`: 152 committed fixtures, two generates identical, `cargo xtask oracle verify` decoded them with libopenmetaverse `a3596616c1c8cf23d2cfb9c71e0b0b4dd36d7d6b`. PR #1, CI https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44049951553 (Linux, Windows, macOS). `cargo xtask oracle verify` stays a manual step; a Linux CI job for it is not added yet. |

XML, binary and notation match libopenmetaverse `a3596616c1c8cf23d2cfb9c71e0b0b4dd36d7d6b`: `tests/oracle_fixtures.rs` decodes every LLSD fixture and re-encodes the round-trip cases to those bytes. `cargo xtask oracle verify` reported 152 fixtures match on Linux. The manager moves the reference checkout when the PR merges. Carry the fixes into Ferroan code that exists now (D7), from the C# at the pin, not from the PR text. That covers at least: (1) the LogoutReply is sent at once in the Unknown category (check the Rust region's logout send path and the fixtures); (2) a circuit taken `ViaLogin` is followed until the avatar is root, so a presence removed first, or no viewer by `[Startup] LoginConnectWaitSeconds` (default 120, 0 keeps the old behaviour), logs that login out and removes its circuit and caps; (3) at region start the region calls GridUser `LoggedOutRegion(regionID)` (the Robust `loggedoutregion` method; an older Robust answers with a failure, which changes nothing); (4) each close handler runs on its own, so one that throws does not stop the presence and GridUser logouts or the removal; (5) whatever #418 and #419 change in the circuit, login and arrival code the Rust region has ported; (6) #429's trusted-host forms in `ferroan-region`'s control-plane parser: an entry is read as an address first, a URI host is used only when it is not empty, an empty name is never looked up, unusable entries are startup warnings, both the `[Network]` and `[Security]` lists are read, names are looked up again at most once a minute after an untrusted call, and IPv6 zones are ignored. D20 rejected the ambiguous forms because C# mishandled them; follow the fixed C# instead, and propose the change to D20's row in this PR for the maintainer. Also #429's `AgentPosition` session check, if the Rust region accepts that update; (7) record what #422 to #426 change for P2.0e (terrain patches, `LayerData`, terrain limits) in that row, and the parcel and land changes in the Phase 3 land rows. Server-side parts (the `loggedoutregion` handler, the SQLite and PostgreSQL store fixes) go into the Phase 1 rows that port those services (P1.3 and the store rows), with a note in each. Acceptance: REFERENCES.md and every manifest record the new pin; the oracles verify; `cargo xtask live run all`, `all grid` and `login grid --region ferroan` pass, locally and in CI from the fork; new tests show (2) to (4) and (6) in the Rust region against fixtures captured from the C# at the pin; `cargo xtask check` passes. Then Q10 resumes. **Evidence:** Implementation in #56 (`chore/pin3`). All 44 commits reviewed in [PIN3_REVIEW.md](docs/PIN3_REVIEW.md); shared reference remains clean at `8583218`. Six C# oracles generate/verify at `40432e0`; the original five retain payload bytes (692 reference, 14 SQLite, 24 config, 58 HTTP, 19 asset cases), with new manifest pins. New actual C# lifecycle/trust observations drive `pending_login_deadline_preserves_last_location_and_releases_caps`, `expired_old_session_does_not_log_out_a_new_grid_login`, `zero_wait_keeps_unopened_login_beyond_client_inactivity`, `connected_and_root_logins_survive_login_deadline_like_csharp`, `startup_cleanup_matches_csharp_and_ignores_old_robust_failure`, `griduser_failure_still_removes_presence_and_keeps_dispatcher_available`, and six control-plane parsing/refresh/resource tests. `cargo xtask check` passes; initial missing README link and stale asset-pin assertion were corrected. Local C# standalone: `capture: 1037 udp, 32 http, check passed, redact passed, replay passed`; C# all grid: `capture: 902 udp, 34 http, check passed, redact passed, replay passed`, neighbour checks pass. Rust login: `root arrival: Presence region and GridUser online/last region/position match current AgentMovementComplete; stored look is zero`, `logout state: GridUser offline, Presence empty; second login and logout pass`, registration/admission/root forms match C#. Fresh circuit, arrival, login, parity, CAPS and root-report captures record Pin3; historical IM and shutdown-kick bytes retain their original capture revision with current source audit. Two ambiguous parity extractions and the isolated-probe fix are recorded under Found while working. | P0.10g | DONE | P2.0d | Harness fixes from the review of #22 (18 November 2026). The findings were not verified: confirm each against the code first, and record here any that turn out to be wrong instead of fixing them. (1) `ferroan-capture http-proxy` gives up on a reply after 5 s (`IO_WAIT` in `crates/ferroan-capture/src/proxy.rs`), while Tranquillity holds an `EventQueueGet` poll for up to 58 s, so grid mode cuts held polls and can lose dequeued events such as `TeleportFinish`. Give each proxy a reply timeout long enough for held polls. Then remove the bot's workarounds, the 8 s poll timeout in `tools/bot/src/caps.rs` and the fixed 1 s settle in `tools/bot/src/scenario.rs`, or record why each stays. The flaky local grid run recorded under Pin2 is the symptom to retest. (2) Grid mode leaves GridServer and both regions running when xtask is interrupted (`process_group(0)`, no state file). Record grid processes so that `cargo xtask live down` stops them, and refuse to start over survivors. (3) `grid::tail` cuts a log to its last 4000 bytes before redacting, so a password across the cut can be printed in part. Redact the whole log first. | P0.10i | DONE | P0.10g, P2.0a2, P2.0b2 | Follow-ups found during Q7 to Q9 (added 18 November 2026, from "Found while working" in WORK_QUEUE.md). (1) LLSD XML binary: `crates/ferroan-llsd/src/xml_llsd.rs::decode_binary` accepts `base16`, unpadded base64 and malformed base64, which becomes empty bytes. Add the malformed cases to the LLSD oracle, match the decoder to it, and check every caller of the decoder. (2) The bot's `Circuit::take_dropped` queue grows until it is drained, and `Session` never drains it. Bound it or consume it, with a test of a long session under heavy loss. (3) A documentation pass: the last paragraph of `crates/ferroan-capture/README.md` says no session capture is committed, but `fixtures/captures/session.cap` exists, and several oracle and session paragraphs in `docs/TESTING.md` describe CI before the live job. Check every guide's claims about CI, captures and fixtures against the current workflows and files. Invalid binary yields whole-document undef through the public C# API; the Rust lower-level decoder returns structured `InvalidBinary`, and `from_xml` maps only that variant to undef. The captured edge cases include required padding, nonzero unused pad bits, interior ASCII versus edge Unicode whitespace, and unsupported encoding accepted only on a self-closing element. Seed array guards therefore return 400 for malformed nested binary. `binary_seed_requests_match_captured_csharp_replies` compares five actual C# request/reply fixtures, status and MIME. Decoder callers were audited: seed, event queue, upload and inventory readers validate required result shapes; generic HTTP LLSD wrappers retain the public undef result; no unrelated error contract changed. | P0.10j | DONE | P0.10a | Trailing blocks Tranquillity sends beyond libopenmetaverse's template (added 18 November 2026, from the second Q10 blocker, #36). TB4 is not a Tranquillity bug: Linden's template (`scripts/messages/message_template.msg` in `~/Git/viewer`, and Firestorm's since 2017) defines `ImprovedInstantMessage`'s `EstateBlock` (Single, `EstateID` U32) and `MetaData` (Variable). `LLClientView.SendInstantMessage` at `8583218` writes `EstateBlock` with `EstateID` 0 and leaves out `MetaData`'s count byte. Ferroan's template is libopenmetaverse's, which lacks both, and [docs/PROTOCOL.md](docs/PROTOCOL.md) "Blocks Aurora adds" chose not to emit them. That choice is wrong wherever Tranquillity sends the blocks. (1) Add the viewer template's trailing blocks to the eight messages in that table, with the viewer template recorded in the crate header and NOTICE.md. Keep libopenmetaverse's message numbers and flags; take nothing else from the viewer template in this row. (2) Make decode and re-encode lossless for a trailing block that is absent: the decoder already reads a missing variable-block count as zero blocks (`Reader::block_count`); record that the count byte was absent, and have the encoder leave it out again, so C#'s 191-byte IM re-encodes to the same 191 bytes. A missing Single block is handled the same way. (3) For each of the eight messages, read what Tranquillity's sender writes at the pin (`LLClientView.cs` and wherever else it is built) and what it reads, and record it in PROTOCOL.md: which blocks C# sends, whether a count byte is present, and what Ferroan sends. Ferroan's own senders write exactly what C# writes. (4) Rewrite PROTOCOL.md's section to match, move TB4 out of docs/TRANQUILLITY_BUGS.md with a note that it was a gap in Ferroan's template, not a C# bug, and regenerate any oracle or fixture output that changes. Acceptance: a C# capture fixture of `ImprovedInstantMessage` re-encodes byte for byte, and the protocol oracle still matches OpenMetaverse `ToBytes` for every message it covered before (a message whose layout changed is compared on the libopenmetaverse prefix and recorded). The preserved Q10 candidate's C# standalone run passes its capture check. `cargo xtask check` passes. **Evidence:** (18 November 2026, #40, `fix/trailing-blocks`): viewer template `ff75ab037607d8fe305f43f8b1f43126693ba2db` contributes only the eight specified suffixes; strict C# oracle validation retains all libopenmetaverse headers and existing blocks at `a3596616c1c8cf23d2cfb9c71e0b0b4dd36d7d6b`. `cargo xtask oracle generate` produced two identical 692-fixture trees; `cargo xtask oracle verify` passed: all 478 original packet files unchanged, 387 `ToBytes` matches, 385 `BuildPacket` successes, 91 without classes, eight additional extension cases compared on raw legacy prefixes. `cargo xtask check` passed. The last uses a real C# 191-byte IM (187-byte prefix plus EstateID zero, absent metadata count); direct fixed-length redaction follows raw-capture lossless validation and pinned C# channel/version evidence. The preserved Q10 code from `789fe59`, applied for acceptance only as local validation commit `5dc069b`, passed C# standalone: `ferrobot login: pass`; `ferrobot connected: pass (120 s; pings and event-queue polls)`; `ferrobot chat receive: pass`; `ferrobot chat send: pass`; `ferrobot IM: pass`; `ferrobot logout: pass (SIGTERM; LogoutReply)`; `capture: 449 udp, 16 http, check passed, redact passed, replay passed`. [CI 44400986944](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44400986944): Linux 3m5s, macOS 3m12s, Windows 4m22s; [live 44400986946](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44400986946) passed in 9m49s. | P0.10k | DONE | P0.10g, P0.10f | Harness and test-evidence fixes (18 November 2026, from the review of #25 to #37 at `ebda8a9`). The findings were checked by the reviewers and the top ones by the manager; confirm each against the code and the C# at the pin first, and record here any that turn out to be wrong instead of fixing them. (1) `xtask/src/grid.rs::prepare_region_cwd` links every publish entry into each grid region's directory, including `OpenSim.db` and the other SQLite files a standalone run leaves, so both grid regions open the same database and inherit the standalone region's prims and estate (seen in `login-arrival/.scratch/live/grid/{home,neighbour}/OpenSim.db`). Give each region its own databases, and make grid results independent of run order. (2) Standalone regions and the asset services (`live.rs`, `assets.rs`) are recorded only after startup succeeds, so Ctrl-C during startup leaves them running and `live down` does not stop them. Record them at spawn, as grid mode does. (3) `ferroan-capture http-proxy` only prints a failed exchange (timeout, chunked reply, oversize, early EOF); count failures and fail the run when any occur. (4) Find the Ferroan binaries through `cargo metadata` or `--message-format=json`, not `target/{debug,release}`, so `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` cannot make the harness run a stale build. (5) Grid recovery aborts if any process has an environment over 64 KiB or the scan exceeds its limits, and then every `live` command fails; skip the oversized entry instead, and document a way out. (6) The proxy treats a `1xx` response as final; skip interim responses. (7) `write_secret` sets mode 0600 only when it creates the file; set it on every write. Test evidence: (8) the event-queue hold test advances the Rust `HOLD` constant and the proxy test passes its own 7 s timeout, so neither would catch a regression to a wrong value: assert `HOLD` against the fixture's `elapsed_ms` and that the proxy's default reply timeout exceeds the hold. (9) Seed fixture tests run through the test-only `CapsServer`; run them through the production `CapsRegistry::router()`. (10) The `connection: close` header and the `/CE/<uuid>` URL shape in the caps tests are hand-written; record them in the fixture manifest from the C# capture. (11) `fixtures/region-login` stores `serde_json` re-serializations; keep the raw scrubbed C# bodies too, or correct the comment at `crates/ferroan-region/src/simulation.rs` that says the formatting comes from the capture. (12) The circuit, arrival and caps fixture generators check provenance by region name and UUID, which the Rust region shares; require a C#-only marker such as the Tranquillity channel string. (13) The `xtask` `grid_state` tests are intermittent on Linux CI: `interrupted_spawn_before_record_is_recovered_without_touching_identical_unmarked_process` ([run 44402778694](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44402778694), environment-open error) and `interrupted_grid_processes_are_refused_on_restart_and_cleaned_by_down` (main push [run 44405166830](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44405166830), `store.down()` at `grid_state.rs:767` returned `another grid command is active` after `drop(run)`). A likely cause, to confirm: tests in the same binary spawn children in parallel, a fork inherits another test's lock descriptor before exec, and the lock paths that only drop the file (the failed `claim()`, for example) leave the `flock` held by that inherited copy; the recovery scan also opens unrelated same-UID processes' environments. Release the lock explicitly on every path, skip unreadable unrelated processes as part (5), include the OS error in recovery failures, and show the fix holds under repeated parallel runs (for example the `grid_state` tests 200 times with the default thread count). Do not disable or serialise tests to hide it. Acceptance: `cargo xtask live run all` followed by `live run all grid` leaves each region with its own database; a Ctrl-C during standalone startup is cleaned up by `live down`; a forced proxy error fails the run; the `grid_state` tests pass 200 repeated parallel runs; `cargo xtask check` passes. **Evidence:** [#44](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/44), `fix/harness-evidence`: all thirteen findings confirmed; inherited-descriptor lock retention reproduced, without claiming a trace of the historical CI interleaving. `cargo xtask check` passes. Tests include `regions_do_not_link_standalone_databases_or_sqlite_sidecars`, `explicit_unlock_releases_inherited_descriptors_on_success_and_error`, `a_forced_upstream_error_fails_the_harness_proxy_run`, artifact selection/custom-target Rust login, and existing-secret mode 0600. Recovery: 200 consecutive default-parallel runs, 2,200 checks in 218s; setup failures and proxy diagnostics recorded in WORK_QUEUE. SIGINT during standalone and asset startup, followed by `live down`, passes. Three regular SQLite stores have distinct inodes after standalone then grid. Fresh C# CAPS hold 58,055ms; 27 client unit plus 4 production-router fixture tests and 13 fixture-generator tests pass. [CI 44421899638](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44421899638): Linux 2m37s, Windows 4m35s, macOS 1m26s. [Live 44421899822](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44421899822), 9m44s: standalone/grid 773/966 UDP and 32/34 HTTP, check/redact/replay passed; neighbour and return teleport pass; grid HTTP 172/0/45/34; register/QueryAccess/CreateAgent/deregister match C#; C#/Rust empty polls 502 at 58,053/58,001ms; assets match 12 HTTP records; persistent texture/inventory and cleanup pass. Firestorm pending. |

| P0.10n8 | DONE (#171) | P0.10n7 | The logout service-state probe in `login grid --rust assets,accounts` stops timing out (added 21 November 2026). `xtask/src/grid_probe.rs` fails with `logout service-state probe exceeded its ten-second deadline` in the `live services` job: four times on 21 November (Q10x, Q22b, Q26a, Q16c; each under "Found while working"), always the `--rust assets,accounts` run, while the accounts-only login in the same job passes. Find out why the probe takes longer than ten seconds there (what it waits for, which service answers late, and whether the cause is in Ferroan, in the C# grid, or in the probe itself), fix the cause, and keep a bound on the wait with a limit id. Do not just raise the deadline unless the evidence shows the work really needs longer. The printed failure is `online` (root arrival after `AgentMovementComplete`), not the logout settle loop: `await_settled` always appends GridUser and Presence flags, and these four failures did not. `post_before` pinned each POST's reqwest timeout to the caller's ten-second deadline, so the first socket that never returned failed the step. On Q16c [run 44963949219](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44963949219) job `137028859895`, the accounts-only login passed. The assets,accounts login completed (`CompleteMovement` in 285 ms, user online, Presence `ReportAgent`). Robust logged no `[LOGHTTP] Slow handling`. About eleven seconds later logout opened a new connection and GridUser offline and Presence `LogoutAgent` were logged in the same millisecond. The probe talks only to Robust's private capture proxy. Ferroan asset and account services do not call back out. One unanswered private POST, GridUser `getgriduserinfo` or Presence `getagent`, spent the budget; the old error omitted the path, so which of the two it was is not in that log. A timeout now names `griduser` or `presence`. Each attempt waits at most one second (`probe.service-attempt`), drops that client, and retries until the same ten-second deadline. The deadline is not raised. `a_stalled_service_post_is_abandoned_inside_one_second` holds the first socket and accepts the retry. Local `cargo xtask check` passed. `live services` on `31282f2` ran `login grid --rust assets,accounts` five times in a row, each `login: pass` with root arrival, at 2026-11-22T05:11:22Z, 05:12:14Z, 05:13:04Z, 05:13:55Z and 05:14:45Z ([run 44987409269](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44987409269), job `137101059478`, 2026-11-22T05:00:10Z–2026-11-22T05:24:28Z). The temporary repeat is removed in this commit. Checks, images, ferrobot, scenarios and the live gate on `31282f2` also passed ([CI](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44987409275), [live](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44987409269)). Firestorm remains pending. |
| P0.10n9 | DONE (#173) | P0.10n8 | A captured UDP packet always re-encodes (added 22 November 2026). The live capture check failed twice on 21 November with `record N (udp in) does not re-encode`: record 162 in `live images` on Q23c and record 197 in `live ferrobot` on Q18a, each passing on the retry. A capture that only sometimes fails to round-trip means the decoder, the encoder or the capture recorder is not deterministic for some packet. Keep the failing capture when it happens (the check should save the record's bytes and message name under `.scratch/` or the job's artifacts), find which message and field it is, and fix the cause in the codec or the recorder. Acceptance: the cause is in the PR with the message name, a fixture of that packet round-trips byte for byte, and the live ferrobot and images jobs pass five runs in a row. **Evidence (22 November 2026, recorded by the manager after the coder's usage ran out):** PR [#173](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/173), `fix/udp-reencode-evidence`, `9b51887`. The two failures were Ferrobot `AgentUpdate` packets: Ferrobot writes a glam-normalised quaternion's x, y, z, and the re-encoder rescaled the decoded quaternion with OpenMetaverse `Quaternion.ToBytes`, which is not a fixed point for every heading. A quaternion already in unpacked form now writes its x, y, z back; `a_viewer_style_agent_update_quaternion_round_trips` requires the same bytes. A mismatch now writes `reencode-mismatch.log` with record index, direction, message and timestamp. The region send path uses the same `pack_quat`, so region packets changed too. CI on `9b51887`, all nine checks green: [checks](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44992842649), [live](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44992842636). The five-runs-in-a-row repeat was not done; the cause, the message and the byte-for-byte fixture are the acceptance met. |
| P0.10n10 | DONE (#192) | P0.10n8 | The private capture proxy never leaves a GridUser POST hanging (added 22 November 2026). #171 bounded each probe attempt to one second and made a timeout name its service; on #172 the logout probe still ran out its ten seconds, naming `griduser` (run 44992751146, job `live services`, 06:24:49Z). Find where that POST stops: the capture proxy's connection handling or forwarding, the C# GridUser handler, or the client. Fix the cause. Acceptance: the cause in the PR, and `live services` green five runs in a row. **Evidence:** The capture file was written on the forwarding path, under the capture mutex, before the client had any response byte. On run 44992751146 the region terrain store did not return for about ten seconds. A capture write in that stall never returned within the one-second probe attempt, so Robust never saw `getgriduserinfo` and the logout probe named `griduser`. The listener stayed up. The proxy queues that request as soon as the bytes are in hand and writes the file on a side thread, so a stalled disk does not hold the response. The response is queued before the client can send the next poll, and the task waits for the disk only after the client has the response. A reset does not fail the proxy. A client that shuts down only its write side still receives the response. No Tranquillity bug row. `proxy::tests` (17 tests) and `a_blocked_capture_write_does_not_stop_the_griduser_probe` passed 20 times in a row, and `cargo xtask check` passed. Five full local `live services` jobs were not held under the lock. On the first CI commit, `live services` passed, including the Rust assets and accounts login. `live ferrobot` and `live images` failed because two separate capture appends let the next event-queue poll land between a request and its reply, and Windows failed because the bounded-response helper was unused when its caller was `cfg(unix)`. Appending each exchange as one pair fixed the split, and the helper is `cfg(unix)`. `live images` failed again on that revision: a quiet event queue holds a poll for about 58 seconds, so a 120-second session finishes two polls and is still inside the third, and the request was absent until the response. The check counts requests and reported too few polls. The held request is now in the capture before the response. Firestorm remains pending. |

| R2 | DONE (#206) | P1.A2 | XAssets never lose an asset to a rolled-back write or a concurrent store (added 22 November 2026, from the manager's review of #114 to #174). (1) #136 `crates/ferroan-services/src/asset/xservice.rs:113-127` `migrate` deletes the chained row after `self.store` returns `Ok`, but the plugins return `Ok` after a rolled-back write (TB27 design), so a failed write (a four-byte character on a `utf8` table, a lock timeout, the race below) loses the only copy; TB33's row promises the asset remains. Check that the row is really there (exists or read back) before the chain delete. (2) #135 `xmysql.rs:123-152` and `xpostgres.rs:150-179`: two stores of the same bytes both see no `XAssetsData` row and both insert; the second hits the primary key, its meta write rolls back, and it reports success; a hash-lookup error is also treated as absent. Use `INSERT IGNORE` on MySQL and `ON CONFLICT (hash) DO NOTHING` on PostgreSQL, and treat a lookup error as an error. Rows and wire unchanged. Record it as a TB row if the C# has the same race (it does: `MySQLXAssetData.cs:279-285`, `PGSQLXAssetData.cs:303-309`). Acceptance: tests for a failed migrate write keeping the chain row, and for two concurrent stores of the same bytes both stored, on both databases. **Evidence:** Pin8 `c401bceecd`. A metadata or data failure still returns from the store with the transaction rolled back. `migrate` reads the asset back and leaves the chain row when that read misses, and this get still returns the chained asset. The chain is SQLite, which keeps a value the XAsset table rejects: MySQL, a name of U+1F600 on the `utf8mb3` table; PostgreSQL, a creator of 129 characters. Both databases: eight pairs of concurrent stores of the same bytes, both ids readable, one data row. `xasset_plugins_match_the_pin` and `xasset_service_matches_the_pin` passed. Local `cargo xtask check` passed. Rows and the HTTP body are unchanged. TB58 and TB59. D43. The live HTTP compare does not run these cases. P1.A2 is unchanged. |

The committed session, circuit, arrival and neighbour captures pass `check_udp`. Local `cargo xtask check` passed. Live ferrobot and live images run in CI. No new TB or D. The P0.10n9 sentence that said the region send path was unchanged is corrected above. |

On `d79b8c5` the same step listed every object and was cancelled at the 45 minute job limit ([run 45460434945](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/45460434945)). On `798eb1b` `git log --raw` still missed blob `8b17374e` ([run 45470072323](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/45470072323)): that `version.json` exists only on merge commit `8614ffeb`, whose parents have different blobs. The ids now come from `git log -m --raw --no-renames`. Rename detection on a blobless clone fetches blobs. GitHub cancels an older pending job in a concurrency group when a newer one queues, even with `cancel-in-progress` false, so two images jobs are not queued by a group. The runner queue keeps both. Before, on main [live run 45340836794](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/45340836794), "Set up the live runner" was 1m21–1m35. | R16 | DONE (this PR) | P0.10q | `cargo xtask docker smoke` runs under rootless Docker (added 22 November 2026, the maintainer's decision on the review of #117). The self-hosted runner's user is being moved off the root-equivalent `docker` group to its own rootless daemon, so that code from any branch cannot use Docker to get root on Home1. Today `xtask/src/docker.rs` runs the grid containers with `--network host` and connects from the host to `127.0.0.1`, which under rootless Docker reaches the rootless namespace, not the host; and it mounts mode-0600 configuration owned by the invoking user, which rootless Docker maps to another uid. Publish each container's ports on `127.0.0.1` instead of host networking (keep the loopback-only binds), make the mounted files readable to the container's mapped user without widening them on the host, and keep the non-root image checks. Work with `DOCKER_HOST` set to the runner user's rootless socket (`unix:///run/user/1001/docker.sock`) and keep the system daemon working too. Acceptance: `cargo xtask docker smoke` passes against both a rootful and a rootless daemon, and the images job passes on Home1 once the maintainer points the runner at the rootless socket. The 22 November note that rootless `--network host` reaches only the rootless namespace does not hold on these Docker 29.10 daemons: the maintainer verified that it uses the host network, so smoke keeps host networking and does not publish ports. **Evidence:** run 45473928673 job 138752986259 built the five images and failed `ferroan-p011-persist has no host pid` because the container had already exited. The cause is the uid map: rootless Docker maps the runner uid to container uid 0, so `--user` with the host uid is a subordinate id and cannot read the mode-0600 mounts. Osiris confirmed it ([run 45497720438](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/45497720438), runner osiris): `rootless owner check: container uid 0 wrote the probe file as host uid 1001` and `docker smoke login+ferrobot: 148s`. Home1 stayed rootful ([run 45497115104](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/45497115104), runner Home1): `rootful daemon: grid user is host uid 1001` and `docker smoke login+ferrobot: 146s`. Smoke uses container uid 0 when `docker info` SecurityOptions contains the token `name=rootless`, and the host uid on a rootful daemon. Before the first grid launch in rootless mode, one `--user 0:0 --network none` container creates a file on a scratch bind mount and smoke refuses unless that file's host owner is the invoking user. Host uid 0 is still refused. The files stay mode 0600. `container_pid` reports status, exit code and the last log lines. Before removal, detached container logs are streamed to `.scratch/live/docker-logs/<name>.log` (at most 64 MiB), which the images artifact already collects via `.scratch/live/**/*.log`. A failed `docker logs` is not copied into that file or into the missing-pid report. Attached `--rm` containers are already gone by then; their output is in the harness log under `.scratch/live/grid`. The first cleanup of a previous run does not save leftover container logs into this run. `deploy/compose/.env` gets the same uid so a later compose up matches, and a file with `RUN_UID=0` must not be reused against a rootful daemon. `otelcol validate` uses `--network none`. Local checks are `cargo xtask check` and a rootful owner-line check. This machine has no rootless daemon. The runner evidence is the osiris and Home1 images jobs above. |

The stored asset description is empty and the inventory item keeps the offer description (`mesh_fee_then_texture_then_the_first_uploader_completes`). The capability body is bounded at 8 MiB (`region.cap-body-bytes`). A body past that is refused and that test's counter moves by one (`a_capability_body_past_8_mib_is_refused`). A body of exactly 8 MiB is accepted and does not move the counter. One agent may hold 64 granted caps, seed names and uploader paths together (`region.agent-caps`). A further uploader path is refused and that test's counter moves by one (`an_agent_past_the_cap_bound_is_refused`). A path that is not a UUID, a path another cap already uses, and an unknown agent do not move it. Another agent can still be granted a path. The comment that a second uploader replaces the first is gone. `uploads` is documented in TESTING.md, LIVE.md and tools/live/README.md. It is a dedicated scenario, so `all` leaves it out. `cargo xtask live run uploads grid --region ferroan` printed `uploads: pass (1127 bytes, two avatars)` and `two roots: QueryAccess and CreateAgent for both harness accounts match the C# fixtures`. `cargo xtask check` passed with the database URLs unset. The 8 MiB capability body and the whole-character cut are known differences (TB82, D67). The live scenario sends a short offer. |

It is longer on CI runners. **Fix:** a hop completion keeps the record root and at rest but holds its actor until the arrival packet samples the pose (`Request::Arrive`), as a login pose already does. The packet and GridUser now report the same landing. Before C# `MakeRootAgent` sends `MoveAgentIntoRegion` with `AbsolutePosition`, it adds the actor and fires the root detectors (`ScenePresence.cs` 1605–1649, 2315–2441 at `c401bceecd`). The neighbour fixture expects that position. No protocol or file format changes. The harness failure line now names each arrival's position and look. **Evidence:** `a_hop_completion_holds_the_landing_until_the_arrival_samples_it` (it fails without the fix: the position moves after three ticks). `a_hop_completion_takes_the_returning_agent_data` now checks that the flying actor starts at the arrival sample. Before the fix, `FERROAN_LIVE_PORT_BASE=23000 cargo xtask live run neighbour grid --region ferroan` under the live lock failed 1 run in 15 with the CI message. After it, 30 consecutive runs passed, each return `AgentMovementComplete` at (128, 128, 30) look (1, 0, 0). `cargo xtask check` passed. Firestorm remains pending. |
| R30 | DONE (this PR) | R29 | Two live-harness flakes (`xtask/` and the capture proxy; no server change). **1. C# landing height.** PR #242's run 45556306815 (live scenarios) failed `neighbour viewer sequence: ... landing 0, AgentMovementComplete 1 (at [128.0, 128.0, 29.967642] look [1.0, 0.0, 0.0])`: the arrival on the C# neighbour. At Pin8 `c401bceecd`, `ScenePresence.CompleteMovement` calls `MakeRootAgent`, which adds the physics actor (`ScenePresence.cs` 1618), before `MoveAgentIntoRegion` samples `AbsolutePosition` (2441). In between, `TriggerOnMakeRootAgent` (1649) makes two synchronous Robust calls on a teleport arrival (`PresenceDetector.cs` 86 `ReportAgent`, `ActivityDetector.cs` 89 `SetLastPosition`), so the avatar falls under ubODE's 9.8 m/s² (`ODEScene.cs` 210) for that wall time, which grows on a slow runner. The observed 0.032 m is about 81 ms of free fall. **Fix:** `compare_neighbour_teleport` takes the server that ran home (`--region ferroan`: Ferroan; the neighbour is always C#); `is_landing` keeps X, Y and the look within 0.001 everywhere and Z within 0.001 on a Ferroan region (QR29 holds the actor), and on a C# region accepts Z in (29.0, 30.001): 1.0 m is about 0.45 s of free fall, five times the observed time, and a landing at ground height (about 25 m) still fails. **2. In that run the neighbour logged the close at 24.035 but the DELETE reached the home proxy at 24.80, after `Grid::stop` had stopped home (deregistered 24.526); the neighbour logged `FAILED: [499] Connection refused`. The scenario cannot await it (a C# side effect the viewer never sees, sent only if the child agent was registered in time). **Fix:** `ProxyStatus::upstream_stopping` (ferroan-capture): a request that arrives after it and finds the upstream gone (connect refused, or `ConnectionReset`, `UnexpectedEof` or `BrokenPipe` before any response byte) stays in the capture with no response, is counted (`refused_after_stop`, printed by the harness) and is not a failure. `Grid::stop` calls `stop_in_order`, which marks each server's proxies (`STOP_ORDER`) just before stopping it. The same errors while the server is meant to be up still fail. **Evidence:** `landing_allows_a_short_fall_on_csharp_regions_only` (exact passes; 29.9676 passes on C#, fails on Ferroan; 30.1 fails on both), `a_refused_upstream_that_is_meant_to_be_up_fails_the_proxy`, `a_request_after_the_upstream_was_stopped_is_not_a_failure`, `an_upstream_closing_before_a_response_fails_only_while_it_is_meant_to_be_up`, `a_partial_response_after_the_mark_still_fails`, `a_refusal_after_the_harness_stops_that_upstream_is_not_a_failure`, and `each_server_is_marked_just_before_it_stops` (it fails if the mark is removed, if `PROXY_HOME` and `PROXY_NEIGHBOUR` or their `STOP_ORDER` entries are swapped, or if the mark comes after the stop). `cargo xtask check` passed. Under the live lock with `FERROAN_LIVE_PORT_BASE=25000`: `cargo xtask live run neighbour grid --region ferroan` passed 10 of 10, and `cargo xtask live run assets grid --region ferroan` passed (the late DELETE did not recur in that run). Firestorm not applicable. |
| R31 | DONE (this PR) | P0.10c | Process-group signals never become `kill -1`. **Cause:** procps-ng 4.0.4's `/usr/bin/kill` (Ubuntu 24.04) reads `kill -TERM -147893` without `--` as a signal to `-1`, so a harness stop on the anubis runner sent `kill(-1, SIGTERM)` and killed every process of the runner user: the job, the runner service and the user's systemd manager with rootless Docker (traced by the maintainer). util-linux `kill` (Arch) parses it correctly, so only Ubuntu runners died. **Fix (xtask only):** `xtask/src/signal.rs` is the one place that runs `kill`: `signal_group` runs `kill -s TERM|KILL -- -<pgid>` and `signal_pid` runs `kill -s TERM -- <pid>`, a form procps-ng and util-linux both parse. Either refuses an id of 0, 1 or above `i32::MAX` before building a command. The 18 direct calls in `live.rs`, `grid.rs`, `grid_state.rs`, `assets.rs`, `auth.rs`, `accounts.rs`, `console_forms.rs` and `ferrobot.rs` use it, keeping each caller's TERM-then-KILL timing, the group-leader guard in `grid_state.rs`, and its error handling. No unsafe code and no new dependency. **Evidence:** `signal::tests`: the exact argv for TERM and KILL; 0, 1 and out-of-range ids refused without running anything; a real Linux test that puts two `sleep` processes in their own groups, asserts the target group is above 1, signals it, and checks that it exits and the other survives; a guard that fails if `Command::new("kill")` (or `/bin/kill`, `/usr/bin/kill`) appears in any other `xtask/src` file. `cargo xtask check` passed. `FERROAN_LIVE_PORT_BASE=26400 UseSharedCompilation=false cargo xtask live run login grid --region ferroan` under the live lock passed twice locally (util-linux 2.42.4); the second run had a logging `kill` shim on `PATH`, which recorded exactly `-s TERM -- -<pgid>` for Robust and the two regions, all of which exited on TERM. No process from the worktree and no listener on ports 26400 to 26409 was left afterwards. A run on an Ubuntu 24.04 runner is pending (CI). |

| P0.13 | DONE (#106) | P0.12 | Logging hooks ([D18](docs/DECISIONS.md)). Ferroan provides the hooks; the operator picks the log system. Every server logs through `tracing`, and: (1) in a container it logs to stdout and stderr, so `docker logs -f` and Docker's logging drivers (journald, fluentd, GELF, syslog) take it as is; (2) with `LOGDIR` set, as Tranquillity's images set it to `/app/logdata`, it also writes rotated files there (`Source/OpenSim.Server.RegionServer/Program.cs` at the pin); (3) `LogFormat = json` gives one JSON object per line with time, level, target, server, region name and id, and agent id where there is one; (4) with `[Monitoring] OtlpEndpoint` set, it exports logs, metrics and traces over OpenTelemetry (OTLP), which any collector can forward to the operator's system. Nothing is exported without the setting; (5) Tranquillity's remote console (`Source/OpenSim.Framework.Console/RemoteConsole.cs`: `/StartSession`, `/SessionCommand`, `/ReadResponses`, `/CloseSession`, with `ConsoleUser` and `ConsolePass`) is served in the same forms, so `OpenSim.ConsoleClient` and web consoles stream the log and run commands. It is off unless configured. Log levels are set per category in the configuration and can be changed at runtime from the console, as with Tranquillity's `CategoryLogLevels` and `set log level`. Redaction: no log line, console reply, JSON field or OTLP record carries a password, session id, secure session id, circuit code or capability URL. The adapter: the compose example runs an OpenTelemetry Collector that takes the servers' OTLP and their container stdout. `deploy/monitoring/collector/` holds working exporter configs for Loki (the example's default, with Grafana), OpenSearch, SigNoz, Graylog (GELF) and Splunk (HEC). Switching systems means choosing a config, not changing Ferroan. Acceptance: `docker smoke` finds `ferroan-bot`'s login in the region's `docker compose logs` output. A JSON line parses with the fields above. Through the collector, the login log line and a login trace reach Loki in `docker smoke`. Each other exporter config passes `otelcol validate`. Only Loki runs in CI. `OpenSim.ConsoleClient` built at the pin reads a Ferroan server's log and runs `show version`. Restored at Pin5 (`c70fee205f`, #465). Pin6's `develop` `e28703a307` includes that fix. P0.13b still runs. The live harness searches each run's logs for the run's generated password, session ids and seed capability path, and finds none. **Blocked (20 November 2026, #106):** `OpenSim.ConsoleClient` at pin `f67db54399` cannot run `show version`. `CommandConsole.FromXml` (`CommandConsole.cs` line 578) indexes `tree["quit"]` before the server help tree. The client registers only `help` (constructor line 728). A valid `StartSession` throws `KeyNotFoundException` and the client prints `Login failed: The given key 'quit' was not present in the dictionary.` Recorded as TB17. Ferroan serves the four forms and does not add a fake quit exchange. **Evidence:** PR #106, `feat/logging`. `crates/ferroan-log` installs `tracing` for `ferroan-region` and `ferroan-robust`. Stdout and stderr are always on. `LOGDIR` writes `ferroan-regionYYYYMMDD.log` or `ferroan-robustYYYYMMDD.log` (UTC). `LogFormat = json` writes `time`, `level`, `target`, `server`, `message`, `region_name`, `region_id`, and `agent_id` when present. `OtlpEndpoint` posts OTLP/HTTP JSON to `/v1/logs`, `/v1/traces` and `/v1/metrics` and is off unless set. A login span is also the log line `trace span=login` so Loki, which stores logs, receives the login trace. `deploy/monitoring/collector/` holds `loki.yaml` (compose default, `grafana/loki:3.2.1` and `otel/opentelemetry-collector-contrib:0.128.0`), `opensearch.yaml`, `signoz.yaml`, `graylog.yaml` (OTLP/gRPC, not GELF; the contrib collector has no GELF exporter) and `splunk.yaml`. Local `otelcol validate` passed for all five. `docker smoke` is the live job: this machine has no Compose plugin, so it was not run here. The remote console matches `RemoteConsole.cs` and is off unless `ConsoleUser` is set. `set log level` changes the floor. Login registers the session id, secure session id, circuit code and seed capability path before the `login accepted` line. The harness scans Ferroan logs for those values. `cargo xtask console-client` builds the pin client into `.scratch` and exits 0 when it sees `Ferroan 0.1.0` or the TB17 `FromXml` error; locally it printed the `FromXml` error. The pin checkout stayed clean. Local `cargo xtask check` passed. CI on this revision is the merge gate. Firestorm remains pending. MySQL and PostgreSQL for `--rust assets` still wait for P1.A1 (#59). **Done (manager, 20 November 2026):** the `show version` sentence was waived until Pin5 restored it; everything else in the acceptance passed in #106. |
| P0.13a | DONE (#108) | P0.13 | Diagnostics (added 19 November 2026, maintainer request: proper readouts when errors happen or limits are hit, so problems can be fixed from the data). (1) **Limit events.** Every bound that refuses, truncates, drops or delays outside input or work reports through one helper: a `tracing` event on target `ferroan::limit` with a stable limit id, soft or hard, the configured value, the observed value, the action taken, and context (server, region, route or packet type, agent id where there is one), plus a `ferroan_limit_hits_total{limit,action}` counter. Soft limits (queues, caches, pools) also report a high-water warning at a configured fraction (default 80%). Reports are rate-limited per limit: the first hit in full, then a count every interval, so a flood cannot flood the log. (2) **Errors.** Structured errors from outside input log their full cause chain and context once, at the boundary that handles them; a malformed packet or body also logs a bounded excerpt (first 256 bytes, hex) at `debug`. (3) **Recent events.** A bounded in-memory ring (default 1000) of warning, error and limit events per server. (4) **`diag dump`.** A console command and `cargo xtask live diag` write a snapshot to `LOGDIR` or `.scratch/`: version and pin, redacted configuration, circuits and their queue depths, limit counters and high-water marks, pool states, and the recent-events ring. (5) **Crashes.** A panic hook writes the backtrace and the ring to `LOGDIR` before exit. (6) **`docs/LIMITS.md`** lists every limit: id, value, where it is, soft or hard, configurable or not, and what C# does at the pin; `cargo xtask check` fails when a limit id in code is missing from it. (7) **Harness.** A live scenario trips at least the POST-body, fetch-folder and one packet limit and asserts the event, the counter and the dump entry; on any live failure the harness collects a `diag dump` from each Rust server, and CI uploads it with the logs. The P0.13 redaction rules apply to all of it. **Evidence (20 November 2026):** [PR #108](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/108), `feat/diagnostics`, implementation `33b030b`. `crates/ferroan-diag` is the one helper. A hit's message contains the limit id, kind, action, configured and observed values, route, server and region. The counter is `ferroan_limit_hits_total{limit,action}`. The first hit is logged in full; later hits within 60 seconds increment the counter and a `suppressed=` summary. Soft high-water warns once at 80% until the value falls. A malformed body logs the first 256 bytes as hex at debug. The ring holds 1000 warning, error and limit events. `diag dump`, `GET /diag` and the panic hook write a redacted snapshot (`diag-<server>-<unix-millis>.txt` or `panic-<server>-<unix-millis>.txt` under `LOGDIR`, or `.scratch/` when `LOGDIR` is unset). `docs/LIMITS.md` lists every id against pin `f67db54399`. `cargo xtask check` fails when an id is missing from that table or has no call site. The live job runs `cargo xtask live run limits grid --region ferroan`: a POST of 1 MiB + 1 from `127.0.0.1` to the region on `127.0.0.2` (status 413), one 4097-byte datagram, and 65 `FetchInventoryDescendents2` folders (status 400). It requires the three log lines, the three refuse series, and the three ids in `GET /diag`, saved as `.scratch/live/diag/limits.txt`. A Ferroan live failure collects `GET /diag` from the region and Robust before the grid stops, and CI uploads the logs and dumps. Local `cargo xtask check` passed. The local `limits` scenario was not run on this machine. CI on this revision is the merge gate. Firestorm remains pending. MySQL and PostgreSQL for `--rust assets` still wait for P1.A1 (#59). |
| P0.10o | DONE | P0.13 | One presence wait before every harness login (added 20 November 2026, after the third "already logged in" refusal: #53, #86, #107). #56 made grid mode wait, between scenarios, until GridUser shows the account offline and Presence has no session for it (`xtask/src/grid_probe.rs`). The asset steps still log in without that wait: "Check the persistent Ferroan asset setup" (#53, #86) and "Compare C# and Ferroan asset services" (#107, [run](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44728950940)) were refused with `login refused (presence): You appear to be already logged in`. Move the wait into the one function every harness login goes through, so no step can log in an account while its last session is still settling; keep it bounded, and when it runs out, fail with the GridUser and Presence state it last saw rather than attempting the login. No retry of a refused login and no fixed sleep. The pin checkout and the reference stay untouched; the patched clone is the harness build only. Record the patch change in `tools/live/README.md` and `docs/LIVE.md`. A missing griduser row is `Failure` from `getgriduserinfo` and counts as offline, so the first login is not held (`a_missing_griduser_row_does_not_block_the_first_login`). Local `cargo xtask check` passed. The live job on `e83b1ed` passed three times in a row: [attempt 1](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44809993818/attempts/1), [attempt 2](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44809993818/attempts/2) and [attempt 3](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44809993818/attempts/3). | P0.10p | DONE | P0.10o | Faster CI (added 20 November 2026 at the maintainer's request). The live job takes about 32 minutes in one sequential job ([run 44785375110](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44785375110): images and docker smoke 7.7 min, Ferrobot 6.9, the harness and event polls 5.5, oracle verify 2.3, about 15 compare and login steps of 30 to 60 s each), and every PR waits for it, docs-only ones included. (1) **Docs-only PRs.** A first step works out whether a pull request changes only documentation: every changed path is Markdown, except files a program reads (today `docs/REFERENCES.md`, which sets the pin and the cache key, `docs/LIMITS.md`, which `cargo xtask check` checks against the code, and `README.md`; find any others with a search of `xtask/src`). Then the live workflow passes in under a minute without building or running anything, and the `checks` jobs run `cargo xtask check --docs-only` instead of the full check. Scheduled and manual runs always run everything. The four required checks keep their names and still report on every PR, so branch protection is unchanged. (2) **Parallel live jobs.** Split the live job into three or four jobs that run at the same time, balanced by the timings above, each doing its own setup from the caches (the Tranquillity build, and the Rust build through a cache action pinned by commit like the others). A final job named `live (ubuntu-latest)` needs all of them and fails unless every one succeeded, so the required check name stays the same. Narrow the Tranquillity cache key to what that build reads (the pin, the capture patch, and the build code), not the whole of `xtask/src/live.rs`, if it can be split cleanly. Keep every step, its order inside its job, and the "Found while working" rules for flakes. Acceptance: a docs-only PR passes all four checks with live under a minute; a code PR's live wall time is recorded before and after and is at most 15 minutes; a failure in any one parallel job fails `live (ubuntu-latest)`; `docs/TESTING.md` and `docs/LIVE.md` describe the new layout. Evidence (#116): before, the single live job on [run 44785375110](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44785375110) was 31 min 41 sec (2026-11-20T17:39:29Z–18:11:10Z). After, [run 44844134712](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44844134712) on `90c806e` was 14 min 28 sec (workflow 2026-11-21T00:29:16Z–00:43:44Z): scope 58 sec, scenarios 9 min 19 sec, Ferrobot 9 min 34 sec, services 11 min 1 sec, images and docker smoke 12 min 39 sec, and the `live (ubuntu-latest)` gate 42 sec. The first Ferrobot job ([run 44839097988](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44839097988)) failed with `run cargo xtask live build first` because standalone Ferrobot does not publish the region; that job now runs `cargo xtask live build` first, and 44844134712 passed. The Tranquillity cache key still hashes `xtask/src/live.rs`, because the publish functions share that file with the scenario runners. `cargo xtask ci scope` and `cargo xtask ci gate` classify the pull request and enforce the required live check. A services login on [run 44841073013](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44841073013) exceeded the ten-second logout probe once; it did not recur on 44844134712. This pull request changes Rust and the workflows, so it is not a documentation-only run. The scope job on this run took 58 sec, including the xtask build. |
| P0.10q | DONE (#117) | P0.10p | Self-hosted runner for the heaviest live job (added 20 November 2026 at the maintainer's request; the maintainer provides an 8-core Arch Linux machine and registers it with the label `ferroan`). After P0.10p splits the live job, only the job that builds the container images and runs `cargo xtask docker smoke` (7.7 min on GitHub, most of it a cold Docker build) runs on that machine, where the Docker layer cache stays warm. Its `runs-on` picks `[self-hosted, linux, x64, ferroan]` only when the repository variable `FERROAN_SELF_HOSTED` is `true` and the run is not from a fork (a scheduled or manual run, or a pull request whose head repository is this one); otherwise `ubuntu-latest`. Pull requests from forks never run on it. Install steps work on both Ubuntu and Arch: skip `apt-get` when the tool is already present. The job removes the containers, networks and volumes it created, and prunes Docker build cache older than seven days, so the machine's disk stays bounded; the runner takes one job at a time because the smoke uses host networking and fixed ports. The required check names do not change. `docs/TESTING.md` says how the runner is set up and how to turn it off (unset the variable). Acceptance: with the variable unset, nothing changes; with it set, the image job runs on the machine on a same-repository PR and on GitHub on a fork PR, and its time on a warm cache is recorded. Evidence (#117): `FERROAN_SELF_HOSTED` was left unset, so this pull request did not set it. On [run 44849353257](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44849353257) (`c00d2ea`, 2026-11-21T01:18:10Z–01:36:15Z) the images job ran on GitHub-hosted `ubuntu-latest` (runner GitHub Actions 1000001708, label `ubuntu-latest`), not on Home1. | P0.13c | DONE (#123) | P0.13a | Logging and diagnostics fixes from the manager's review of #103 to #112 (20 November 2026). Each was checked against the code. (1) **Secrets in the diagnostics ring.** `ferroan_diag::log_malformed` (`crates/ferroan-diag/src/lib.rs`) puts a hex excerpt of the raw body into the ring through `take_log` at any log level, and redaction cannot match hex. A malformed `/agent` CreateAgent body (`crates/ferroan-region/src/simulation.rs`) or an oversize UseCircuitCode (`crates/ferroan-client/src/circuit.rs`) puts session ids, the secure session id, the circuit code and the seed capability path into `/diag`, `diag dump` files and crash reports. Keep the excerpt out of the ring and the dump files: it goes only to the debug log line, and only after the known secret fields of that message type are masked; the ring keeps route, length and the parse error. (2) **Redaction list without a bound.** `SECRETS` in `crates/ferroan-log/src/redact.rs` gains four entries per login and is never pruned, and every log line scans all of them under one mutex. Forget a session's secrets when it closes, bound the list with a limit id in `docs/LIMITS.md`, and report a hit through the diagnostics helper. (3) **Robust HTTP metrics.** `record` in `crates/ferroan-robust/src/status.rs` keys rows by the raw path and stops adding at 128, so unauthenticated junk paths fill the table and real routes stop being counted. Label by the matched route (unmatched requests share one `other` row), not the raw path. (4) **Remote console sessions.** `crates/ferroan-log/src/console.rs` never expires a session. `RemoteConsole.cs` at the pin drops a connection idle for more than 500 seconds (`DoExpire`); do the same, and bound the number of sessions with a limit id. An empty `ConsolePass` accepting an empty `PASS` matches C# and stays. (5) **Structured fields dropped.** `EventFields::store` in `crates/ferroan-log/src/layer.rs` keeps only `message` and `agent_id`, so `error = %e` and fields from dependencies vanish from every output. Keep every field: appended as `key=value` in text and as JSON members, redacted like the message. (6) **Smaller ones.** The `ferroan_region_udp_unacked` gauge is only set in the resend branch and is not cleared when a circuit ends; make it the sum over open circuits. `Budget::charge` in `crates/ferroan-llsd/src/lib.rs`, and `drop_bound` in the reliability code, report the failing charge rather than the total that crossed the bound as `observed`. `catalog_ids()` and `static_limit()` in `crates/ferroan-diag/src/lib.rs` re-list the catalogue by hand; count `hit.id` directly so a new limit cannot be missed. Acceptance: a unit test for each, including a malformed CreateAgent body whose session id appears in no ring line, `diag dump` or panic file; `cargo xtask check`; the live job passes. Next action: the manager splits this row and keeps part (1) first. The proposed split is under Q13c in WORK_QUEUE.md. No code was changed. Evidence (#123): parts (1), (2) and (4) are DONE in #121 and #122. Parts (3), (5) and (6) are DONE on P0.13c3. `cargo xtask check` passed locally. CI https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44869114069 and the live run https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44869114040 passed on `3163dd5`. Live images ran on Home1 from 2026-11-21T05:04:55Z to 2026-11-21T05:10:18Z. Firestorm remains pending. |

A harness step speaks the four forms of `Source/OpenSim.Framework.Console/RemoteConsole.cs` over HTTP, as the client would: `/StartSession` with `ConsoleUser` and `ConsolePass`, `/SessionCommand` with `show version`, `/ReadResponses` until the reply arrives, and `/CloseSession`. The step runs in the live job. When a pin move carries the TB17 fix, restore the `OpenSim.ConsoleClient` sentence in P0.13 and keep this step. **Blocked (21 November 2026, #124):** a local compare at pin `c70fee205f` spoke the four forms to the C# region (`--console rest`) and to `ferroan-region` with the same console login. Strict element, attribute and order compare fails, and the failures are not only the wildcards the row names. StartSession is `SessionID`, `Prompt`, `HelpTree` on both, but the prompt is `Region (Ferroan Test) # ` against `Ferroan# ` and the help trees are 217 commands against 4. After `show version`, C# returns three `Line` elements (the input echo `show version`, `Version: …`, then the prompt) and Ferroan returns one `Info` line `0:0:Ferroan 0.1.0 (ferroan-region)`. A parsed unknown session id on `CloseSession` is 200 `text/xml` `Result OK` on C# and 404 `text/plain` on Ferroan. The same id on `ReadResponses` is 200 `text/html` with an empty body on C# (no poll handler; the HTTP server sends its default) and 404 `text/plain` on Ferroan. Wrong password, a known `SessionCommand`, a known `CloseSession` and an unknown `SessionCommand` already match. The startup `ReadResponses` was drained and not compared. No reply carried the password. No code was committed: a live step with this strict compare would fail CI. The proposed acceptance is under Q13b in WORK_QUEUE.md. Do not port the command catalogue and do not rename Ferroan's prompt to `Region`. **Retargeted (21 November 2026, manager, with the maintainer's decisions):** the acceptance is now the Q13b retarget note in WORK_QUEUE.md. The prompt comes from `ConsolePrompt` with C#'s default and is compared exactly; an unknown session id on `CloseSession` and `ReadResponses` matches C#'s status and body exactly, recorded as a TB row; `show version` emits C#'s three lines; only the `HelpTree` children, the version text and the line number are wildcarded. **Evidence:** [PR #131](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/131), `feat/console-forms`, pin `c70fee205f`. An unknown parsed session id is `CloseSession` OK and `ReadResponses` 200 `text/html` empty (TB31). `OpenSim.ConsoleClient` still drops those lines (TB32); the in-process console adds one `0:0:` line after the three, and the region replies do not. Wildcards are the `HelpTree` children, the version text and the line number. | P2.0a2 | DONE | P2.0d | Circuit fixes from the review of #20 (18 November 2026). The reviewer read a Tranquillity checkout that was not the pin, so confirm each finding at the pin first, and record here any that turn out to be wrong. (1) `RegionHandshake` sends what `LLClientView.SendRegionHandshake` sends, filled from the region's configuration: the `RegionInfo4` block (`RegionFlagsExtended`, `RegionProtocols`), `CacheID`, `SimName`, `SimAccess`, `WaterHeight`, `ProductName` and the rest. Today `RegionInfo4` is empty and those fields are defaults. (2) Immediate `PacketAck`s take reliable sequence numbers. They must not, if C# sends them outside the sequence (`SendAckImmediate`). (3) A UDP receive or send error for one peer must not end the circuit. That includes `ConnectionReset` on Windows, which `OpenSimUDPBase` avoids by turning `SIO_UDP_CONNRESET` off. (4) A reliable packet that has used up its resends stays in the unacked window, and once 256 are stuck every later send fails with `TooManyUnacked`. Drop it, or drop the client as C# does. (5) The agent, session and circuit-code checks (`session_packet`) run, but their result is thrown away. Act on them, or remove them. (6) Send `LogoutReply` as C# does: reliable or not, and whether the server waits before closing. (7) Move the reliability code shared with `tools/bot/src/circuit.rs` into one place. (8) Bring the `circuit.rs` header to PORTING.md form at the pin. Acceptance: `RegionHandshake`, `PacketAck` sequencing and `LogoutReply` match a C# region's capture byte for byte, with IDs folded as in P0.10d, and `cargo xtask live run login grid --region ferroan` still passes. Pin review: `SendAckImmediate` bypasses the sequence counter only for initial/pending `UseCircuitCode`; established ACKs allocate ordinary sequences and batch up to 255 IDs. `LogoutReply` was already reliable and zero-coded, and C# closes without waiting for its ACK; new captured-byte tests prove that behavior. C# has no four-retry cutoff, instead retrying until client inactivity; Ferroan keeps its bounded four-retry policy but now removes exhausted entries, as this row permits. Discarded movement/throttle/height circuit-code stubs were removed, and implemented packet agent/session checks now run before appended ACKs. Handshake configuration uses region INI name/type, configured estate owner and pinned fresh-region settings; persisted region/estate stores remain planned. `cargo xtask check` passed locally, including `initial_ack_and_configured_handshake_match_csharp_datagrams`, `queued_ack_matches_csharp_bytes_and_consumes_a_sequence`, `logout_send_path_matches_csharp_bytes_and_finishes_without_ack`, `receive_and_send_peer_errors_do_not_end_the_circuit`, `failed_reliable_send_is_retained_and_retried` and shared-window exhaustion tests. PR [#31](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/31), ordinary CI [run 44275172507](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44275172507) (Ubuntu 2m15s, macOS 3m24s, Windows 4m31s), live [run 44275174613](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44275174613) (6m36s) on `ae8a0cf`. Local and CI `cargo xtask live run login grid --region ferroan` printed `login: pass`, `live: run passed`, `logout: LogoutReply` and `grid login http: register, QueryAccess, CreateAgent, deregister match C#`; CI printed `grid http: robust-private 9, robust-public 0, home 4, neighbour 0`. Final evidence-revision checks are required before auto-merge and linked in the PR. No viewer check. |
